By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e436f6e73747261696e742e616464496e657175616c6974795f736174}, we obtain \(\operatorname{sat^{\prime}}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(-(-1)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{none}),\allowbreak{} \langle 0\rangle,\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \mathrm{true}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e746964795f736174}, we obtain \(\operatorname{sat^{\prime}}(\operatorname{tidy\,Constraint}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(1),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{none}),\allowbreak{} \langle 0\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{tidy\,Coeffs}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(1),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{none}),\allowbreak{} \langle 0\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \mathrm{true}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e436f6e73747261696e742e6e6f745f736174275f6f665f6973496d706f737369626c65}, we obtain \(\mathrm{False}\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(n_{3} < n_{3} + 1 + 1\right)\).
By definition, we obtain \(\mathrm{true} = \mathrm{true}\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(\operatorname{decide}(\operatorname{is\,Impossible}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(1),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{some}(0))) = \mathrm{true}) = \mathrm{true}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6f665f6465636964655f65715f74727565}, we obtain \(\operatorname{is\,Impossible}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(1),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{some}(0))) = \mathrm{true}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\neg \left(n_{3} < n_{3} + 1 + 1\right)\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c655f6f665f6e6f745f6c74}, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 + 1 \le n_{3}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e496e742e6f664e61745f6c655f6f665f6c65}, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 + 1 \le n_{3}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e7375625f6e6f6e6e65675f6f665f6c65}, we obtain \(0 \le n_{3} - \left(n_{3} + 1 + 1\right)\).
Using Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e4c696e656172436f6d626f2e636f6f7264696e6174655f6576616c5f30} in reverse gives \(n_{3} = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{coordinate}(0),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e6f664e61745f616464} with \(n_{3} + 1\) in place of its parameter \(n\) and \(m = 1\), we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 + 1 = n_{3} + 1 + 1\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e6f664e61745f616464} with \(n_{3}\) in place of its parameter \(n\) and \(m = 1\), we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 = n_{3} + 1\).
Using Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e4c696e656172436f6d626f2e636f6f7264696e6174655f6576616c5f30} in reverse gives \(n_{3} = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{coordinate}(0),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By definition, we obtain \(1 = 1\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e496e742e6164645f636f6e6772}, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{coordinate}(0),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) + \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Using Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e4c696e656172436f6d626f2e6164645f6576616c} with \(l_{1} = \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle)\) and \(l_{2} = \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle)\) and \(v = \operatorname{of\,List}\left(\langle n_{3}\rangle\right)\) in reverse gives \(\operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) + \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By definition, we obtain \(1 = 1\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e496e742e6164645f636f6e6772}, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 + 1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) + \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Using Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e4c696e656172436f6d626f2e6164645f6576616c} with \(l_{1} = \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle)\) and \(l_{2} = \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle)\) and \(v = \operatorname{of\,List}\left(\langle n_{3}\rangle\right)\) in reverse gives \(\operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) + \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 + 1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \(n_{3} + 1 + 1 = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e496e742e7375625f636f6e6772}, we obtain \(n_{3} - (n_{3} + 1 + 1) = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{coordinate}(0),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) - \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) + \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(1,\allowbreak{} \langle\,\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Using Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e4c696e656172436f6d626f2e7375625f6576616c} with \(l_{1} = \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle)\) and \(l_{2} = \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(2,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle)\) and \(v = \operatorname{of\,List}\left(\langle n_{3}\rangle\right)\) in reverse gives \(\operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) - \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(2,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) - \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(2,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \(n_{3} - (n_{3} + 1 + 1) = \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) - \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(2,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6c655f6f665f6c655f6f665f6571} with \(\alpha = \mathbb{Z}\), we obtain \(0 \le \operatorname{eval}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(0,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle) - \operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Linear\,Combo\mathord{:}mk}(2,\allowbreak{} \langle 1\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle))\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e436f6e73747261696e742e616464496e657175616c6974795f736174}, we obtain \(\operatorname{sat^{\prime}}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(-(-2)),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{none}),\allowbreak{} \langle 0\rangle,\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \mathrm{true}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e746964795f736174}, we obtain \(\operatorname{sat^{\prime}}(\operatorname{tidy\,Constraint}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(2),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{none}),\allowbreak{} \langle 0\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{tidy\,Coeffs}(\operatorname{Lean\mathord{:}Omega\mathord{:}Constraint\mathord{:}mk}(\operatorname{some}(2),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{none}),\allowbreak{} \langle 0\rangle),\allowbreak{} \operatorname{of\,List}(\langle n_{3}\rangle)) = \mathrm{true}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c65616e2e4f6d6567612e436f6e73747261696e742e6e6f745f736174275f6f665f6973496d706f737369626c65}, we obtain \(\mathrm{False}\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m < n_{3} + 1 + 1 \implies |\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(m))| = D(m)\), Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:446563696461626c652e6279436f6e74726164696374696f6e}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6d756c5f616464}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:446973747269622e6c65667444697374726962436c617373}, Lemma~\ref{theorem:2}, Lemma~\ref{theorem:5} and the two auxiliary results established above gives \(|\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(n_{3} + 1 + 1))| = D(n_{3} + 1 + 1)\).
\end{proofstep}
Use the hypothesis \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m < n_{2} + 1 \implies |\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(m))| = D(m)\).
The zero and successor cases exhaust the natural numbers. Hence \(\left|\operatorname{derangements}\left(\operatorname{Fin}(n_{2} + 1)\right)\right| = D\left(n_{2} + 1\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
Use the hypothesis \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m < n_{1} \implies |\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(m))| = D(m)\).
The zero and successor cases exhaust the natural numbers. Hence \(\left|\operatorname{derangements}\left(\operatorname{Fin}(n_{1})\right)\right| = D\left(n_{1}\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
By strong induction on the natural number, we obtain \(\left|\operatorname{derangements}\left(\operatorname{Fin}(n)\right)\right| = D\left(n\right)\).

\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[{Cardinality derangements equals num derangements}]\label{theorem:8}
Let \(\alpha\) be a type. Fix a finite enumeration of \(\alpha\). Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle \left|\operatorname{derangements}\left(\alpha\right)\right| = D\left(\left|\alpha\right|\right).\)
\end{center}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f66696e}, we have \(\left|\operatorname{Fin}(\left|\alpha\right|)\right| = \left|\alpha\right|\).
By Lemma~\ref{theorem:1} applied to \(\left|\operatorname{Fin}(\left|\alpha\right|)\right| = \left|\alpha\right|\), we have \(|\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(|\alpha|))| = |\operatorname{derangements}(\alpha)|\).
Substituting by Lemma~\ref{theorem:1} applied to \(\left|\operatorname{Fin}(\left|\alpha\right|)\right| = \left|\alpha\right|\) reduces the claim to \(|\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(|\alpha|))| = D(|\alpha|)\).
By Lemma~\ref{theorem:7}, we obtain \(|\operatorname{derangements}(\operatorname{Fin}(|\alpha|))| = D(|\alpha|)\).

\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[{Num derangements sum}]\label{theorem:9}
Let \(n\) be a natural number. Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle D(n) = \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} n - k)).\)
\end{center}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Proceed by induction on \(n\).
\emph{Base case.}
\begin{proofstep}
At zero, \(D(0) = \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<0 + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} 0 - k))\) holds by definition. Their common value is \(1\) by Theorem~\ref{theorem:3}.
\end{proofstep}
\emph{Successor step.}
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n_{1}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(D(n_{1}) = \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - k))\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume \(x \in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}\).
By Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f72616e67655f737563635f696666}, we obtain \(x \in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\} \iff x \le n_{1}\).
Use the hypothesis \(x \in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}\).
By the forward implication, we obtain \(x \le n_{1}\).
By definition, we obtain \((-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e636173745f6f6e65} in the preceding result gives \((-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6d756c5f6c6566745f636f6d6d} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e6e6174436173745f616464} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e6e6174436173745f6d756c} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6164645f747375625f63616e63656c5f6f665f6c65} (using \(x \le n_{1}\)), Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:43616e6f6e6963616c6c794f7264657265644164642e746f4578697374734164644f664c45}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e737443616e6f6e6963616c6c794f726465726564416464}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:49734f7264657265644164644d6f6e6f69642e746f4164644c6566744d6f6e6f}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e737449734f7264657265644164644d6f6e6f6964} and Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e73744f726465726564537562} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((x + (n_{1} - x) + 1) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6164645f72696768745f636f6d6d} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot ((x + 1 + (n_{1} - x)) \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e617363466163746f7269616c5f73756363} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x + 1)\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e737563635f737562} (using \(x \le n_{1}\)) in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - x)) = (-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(\sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e73756d5f636f6e6772} and the identity established above in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6164645f6c6566745f696e6a}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:497343616e63656c4164642e746f4973526967687443616e63656c416464}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:49734f72646572656443616e63656c4164644d6f6e6f69642e746f497343616e63656c416464}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:49735374726963744f72646572656452696e672e746f49734f72646572656443616e63656c4164644d6f6e6f6964} and Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e696e737449735374726963744f72646572656452696e67} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) + -(-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + -(-1)^{n_{1}}\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:7375625f65715f6164645f6e6567} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + -(-1)^{n_{1}}\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6e65675f6f6e655f6d756c} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + -1 \cdot (-1)^{n_{1}}\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:706f775f7375636327} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1}\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6d756c5f6f6e65} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot 1\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:496e742e6f664e61745f6f6e65} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot 1\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e617363466163746f7269616c5f7a65726f} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(n_{1} + 1 + 1,\allowbreak{} 0)\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:747375625f73656c66}, Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e737443616e6f6e6963616c6c794f726465726564416464} and Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e696e73744f726465726564537562} in the preceding result gives \(\sum_{i\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((n_{1} + 1) \cdot ((-1)^{i} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(i + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - i))) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(n_{1} + 1 + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - (n_{1} + 1))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d756c5f73756d} in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - k)) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(n_{1} + 1 + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - (n_{1} + 1))\).
Rewriting with \(D(n_{1}) = \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} - k))\) in the preceding result gives \((n_{1} + 1) \cdot D(n_{1}) - (-1)^{n_{1}} = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(n_{1} + 1 + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - (n_{1} + 1))\).
Rewriting with Lemma~\ref{theorem:6} in the preceding result gives \(D(n_{1} + 1) = \sum_{x\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1\}}((-1)^{x} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(x + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - x)) + (-1)^{n_{1} + 1} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(n_{1} + 1 + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - (n_{1} + 1))\).
Rewriting with Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e73756d5f72616e67655f73756363} in the preceding result gives \(D(n_{1} + 1) = \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n_{1} + 1 + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} n_{1} + 1 - k))\).
\end{proofstep}
By induction, \(D(n) = \sum_{k\in \{i\in\mathbb{N}\mid i<n + 1\}}((-1)^{k} \cdot \operatorname{asc\,Factorial}(k + 1,\allowbreak{} n - k))\).

\end{proof}
